### Observations against the standing rules (not internal conflicts; nothing changed here)

- **Owner licence intent.** `docs/OWNER-INSTRUCTIONS-2026-09-25.md` records the owner saying "I intend to make this module … GPL three license". §17.3 and D-22 keep a proprietary default "until the owner chooses" and never mention that stated intent. This pass may not change the decision; the owner or Fable should rule whether §17.3 / D-22 now record the GPL-3 choice (the permissive-only dependency rule is compatible with either).
- **No-defer wording.** No normative deferral was found in this part. The words that a wording check will flag are all in history or quotations: §0.6 "the deferrals" and "CityJSON to the deferrals" (history, contradicted by the body — SCOPE-C10), §0.7 "the next amendment pass's input" (history), §0.5 "OPEN" for mechanisms the corpus leaves unrecorded (a statement about the corpus, not a deferral — reword as "unrecorded" if the checker is literal), and the Staged section's "former Deferrals list" (history of a closed list).
- **Clean-room.** The part quotes no reference code, grammar, palette or word list; reference numbers appear as calibration anchors with provenance, as §17.1 (6) allows. §17.1 (2)'s document checks bind the Assemble seat that writes the consolidated `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`; moving the judgment calls to `docs/ARCHITECTURE-RATIONALE.md` must keep every "J-…" and "§n" pointer resolvable (check (v) resolves only §-pointers within one document).
- **Rounding, not conflicts:** the summary's "about 59 000" and "46 000" people round §1.1's 58 800 and 45 800; §1.4 gives FTG's entrance as 3 m, §13.5 as 2.99 m; §1.4's determinism row names six stream-key components, D-15 all eight (seed key and salt added); §0.2 and Appendix A.2 describe the same reading at different granularity. The consolidated text should keep only the canonical figure.
